    function CheesyReadUserData()
        local table_0031 = {}
        if not (isfile and readfile) then
            return table_0031
        end
        helper_0014()
        local pcall_success_0032, local_0053 = pcall(readfile, "cheesy/data/userdata.json")
        if not pcall_success_0032 or type(local_0053) ~= "string" or local_0053 == "" then
            return table_0031
        end
        local pcall_success_0033, local_0054 = pcall(function()
            return service_http_service_0011:JSONDecode(local_0053)
        end)
        if pcall_success_0033 and type(local_0054) == "table" then
            return local_0054
        end
        return table_0031
    end
    function CheesyWriteUserData()
        if not (writefile and isfile) then
            warn("[cheesy] writefile/isfile unavailable on this executor, nothing can be saved")
            return
        end
        helper_0014()
        local local_0055 = CheesyReadUserData()
        local_0055.Language = CheesyLanguage
        local_0055.LanguageVersion = CheesyLanguageVersion
        local_0055.SkipLanguagePrompt = CheesySkipLanguagePrompt
        local pcall_success_0034, local_0056 = pcall(function()
            writefile("cheesy/data/userdata.json", service_http_service_0011:JSONEncode(local_0055))
        end)
        if not pcall_success_0034 then
            warn("[cheesy] userdata write FAILED: " .. tostring(local_0056))
            return
        end
    end

    function CheesyLoadTranslations()
        CheesyTranslations = nil
        if not CheesyLanguage or CheesyLanguage == "english" then
            return
        end
        local local_0058 = loadstring
        if not local_0058 and (RunService and RunService.LoadString) then
            local_0058 = RunService.LoadString
        end
        if not local_0058 then
            return
        end
        local pcall_success_0035, local_0059 = pcall(function()
            local downloaded_source_0036 = game:HttpGet(
                CheesyLanguageBase
                    .. ("/" .. (CheesyLanguage .. (".lua?nb=" .. tostring(math.floor(tick())))))
            )
            local local_0060 = (local_0058(downloaded_source_0036))()
            if type(local_0060) == "table" then
                CheesyTranslations = local_0060
                CheesyBuildTranslateIndex(local_0060)
            end
        end)
        if not pcall_success_0035 then
            warn(

    end
    function CheesyBuildTranslateIndex(param_0028)
        CheesyConcatWords = {}
        CheesyWordIndex = {}
        local function helper_0015(param_0029, param_0030, param_0031)
            local local_0061 = param_0029[param_0030]
            if not local_0061 then
                local_0061 = {}
                param_0029[param_0030] = local_0061
            end
            table.insert(local_0061, param_0031)
        end
        for item_0013 in pairs(param_0028) do
            local local_0062 = string.match(item_0013, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if type(local_0062) == "string" and local_0062 ~= "" then
                local local_0063 = string.match(local_0062, "^(%S+)")
                if local_0063 then
                    helper_0015(CheesyWordIndex, local_0063, local_0062)
                    if local_0062 ~= item_0013 then
                        helper_0015(CheesyConcatWords, local_0063, local_0062)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function CheesyTranslate(param_0032)
        if type(param_0032) ~= "string" or not CheesyTranslations then
            return nil
        end
        local local_0064 = CheesyTranslations[param_0032]
        if type(local_0064) == "string" and local_0064 ~= "" then
            return local_0064
        end
        local local_0065 = string.match(param_0032, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if local_0065 ~= param_0032 then
            local_0064 = CheesyTranslations[local_0065]
            if type(local_0064) == "string" and local_0064 ~= "" then
                return local_0064
            end
        end
        local local_0066 = param_0032:find("✅", 2657109421 % 16401910, true)
            or param_0032:find("❌", 379628 + -379627, true)
        if local_0066 then
            local local_0067 = param_0032:sub(67043 - 67042, local_0066 - (63203 - 63202))
            local local_0068 = string.match(local_0067, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if local_0068 ~= "" and local_0068 ~= local_0067 then
                local local_0069 = CheesyTranslate(local_0068)
                if local_0069 and (type(local_0069) == "string" and local_0069 ~= "") then
                    local local_0070 = local_0067:sub(#local_0068 + 829409441 % 3606128)
                    return local_0069 .. (local_0070 .. param_0032:sub(local_0066))
                end
            end
        end
        if CheesyConcatWords then
            local table_0037 = {}
            local local_0071 = 879803 - 879803
            for item_0014 in string.gmatch(param_0032, "%S+") do
                table.insert(table_0037, {
                    ["Text"] = item_0014,
                    ["Start"] = local_0071 + 1,
                    ["Stop"] = local_0071 + #item_0014,
                })
                local_0071 = (local_0071 + #item_0014) + (-934077 + 934078)
            end
            local local_0072 = table_0037[906055865 % 9245468]

                for item_0015, item_0016 in ipairs(CheesyConcatWords[local_0072.Text] or {}) do
                    if param_0032:sub(609588 - 609587, #item_0016) == item_0016 then
                        local local_0073 =
                            param_0032:sub(#item_0016 + (-400428 + 400429), #item_0016 + 1)
                        if local_0073 == "" or local_0073 == " " then
                            local local_0074 = CheesyTranslations[item_0016]
                                or CheesyTranslations[item_0016 .. " "]
                            if type(local_0074) == "string" and local_0074 ~= "" then

                                    local_0074:sub(-1) == " "
                                    and local_0075:sub(57481975 % 2612817, 1) == " "
                                then
                                    local_0075 = local_0075:sub(46654898 % 2915931)
                                end
                                return local_0074 .. local_0075
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            local local_0076 = table_0037[#table_0037]
            if local_0076 and #table_0037 > 640465135 % 7447269 then
                for item_0017, item_0018 in ipairs(CheesyConcatWords[local_0076.Text] or {}) do
                    if #param_0032 >= #item_0018 and param_0032:sub(-#item_0018) == item_0018 then
                        local local_0077 =
                            param_0032:sub(382199281 % 6369988, #param_0032 - #item_0018)
                        if local_0077 ~= "" and local_0077:sub(-272488 + 272487) == " " then
                            local local_0078 = CheesyTranslations[item_0018]
                                or CheesyTranslations[" " .. item_0018]
                            if type(local_0078) == "string" and local_0078 ~= "" then
                                return local_0077 .. local_0078
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            for item_0019 = 60745553 % 3796597, #table_0037, -315370 + 315371 do
                local local_0079 = CheesyWordIndex
                    and CheesyWordIndex[table_0037[item_0019]["Text"]]

                        if #item_0021 > #(local_0080 or "") then

                            local local_0084 = param_0032:sub(1, local_0082 - (775352 + -775351))

                                local_0083 + (485743 + -485742),

                            )
                            local local_0086 = local_0084 == ""
                                or local_0084:sub(-194598 + 194597) == " "
                                or local_0084:sub(929981 + -929982) == "["

    })
    AntiLagOriginal = setmetatable({}, { ["__mode"] = "k" })
    AntiLagConn = nil
    function AntiLagApplyPart(param_0060)
        if not param_0060 or not param_0060:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end
        if AntiLagOriginal[param_0060] == nil then
            AntiLagOriginal[param_0060] = param_0060.Material
        end
        if param_0060.Material ~= Enum.Material["Plastic"] then
            param_0060.Material = Enum.Material["Plastic"]
        end
    end
    function StopAntiLag()
        if AntiLagConn then
            AntiLagConn:Disconnect()
            AntiLagConn = nil
        end
        for item_0063, item_0064 in pairs(AntiLagOriginal) do
            if item_0063 and item_0063.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    item_0063.Material = item_0064
                end)
            end
        end
        AntiLagOriginal = setmetatable({}, { ["__mode"] = "k" })
        local child_instance_0084 = workspace:FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
        if child_instance_0084 then
            for item_0065, item_0066 in ipairs(child_instance_0084:GetDescendants()) do
                if item_0066:IsA("BasePart") and item_0066:GetAttribute("Mat") ~= nil then
                    item_0066:SetAttribute("Mat", nil)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local_0122.AntiLag:OnChanged(function(param_0061)
        if not param_0061 then
            StopAntiLag()
            return
        end
        local child_instance_0085 = workspace:FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
        if child_instance_0085 then
            for item_0067, item_0068 in ipairs(child_instance_0085:GetDescendants()) do
                AntiLagApplyPart(item_0068)
            end
        end
        if not AntiLagConn then
            AntiLagConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(param_0062)
                if _Unloading then
                    return
                end
                if not (local_0122.AntiLag and local_0122.AntiLag["Value"]) then
                    return
                end
                AntiLagApplyPart(param_0062)
            end)
        end
    end)

    OrbitConn = nil
    local function helper_0038()
        if OrbitConn then
            OrbitConn:Disconnect()
            OrbitConn = nil
        end
        if not local_0122.OrbitItems["Value"] then
            return
        end
        if not local_0115 or not local_0117 then
            return
        end
        OrbitConn = service_run_service_0047.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if _Unloading then
                return
            end
            if not local_0122.OrbitItems["Value"] or not local_0115 or not local_0117 then
                return
            end
            if CurrentFloor == "Fools" then
                return
            end
            if tick() < (ScrapDroppedItemsHoldUntil or 685955543 % 16730623) then
                return
            end
            local child_instance_0092 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Drops")
            if not child_instance_0092 then
                return
            end
            local children_0093 = child_instance_0092:GetChildren()
            local local_0187 = #children_0093
            if local_0187 == 2921255645 % 11546465 then
                return
            end
            local local_0188 = tick()
            local local_0189 = 219047 + -219043
            for item_0077, item_0078 in ipairs(children_0093) do
                if item_0078:IsA("Model") then
                    local local_0190 = local_0188 * local_0123.OrbitSpeed["Value"]
                        + ((item_0077 / local_0187) * math.pi) * (666050822 % 3917946)
                    local local_0191 = Vector3.new(
                        math.cos(local_0190) * local_0189,
                        -local_0123.OrbitYOffset["Value"],
                        math.sin(local_0190) * local_0189
                    )
                    item_0078:PivotTo(CFrame.new(local_0117.Position + local_0191))
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    BringItemsBVs = {}
    function ClearBringItemsForces()
        for item_0079, item_0080 in ipairs(BringItemsBVs) do
            if item_0080.Parent then
                pcall(item_0080.Destroy, item_0080)

    function StopBringItems()
        if BringItemsConn then
            BringItemsConn:Disconnect()
            BringItemsConn = nil
        end
        ClearBringItemsForces()
    end
    function ResolveDropItem(param_0067)
        if param_0067:IsA("BasePart") then
            return param_0067
        end
        if param_0067:IsA("Model") then
            return param_0067.PrimaryPart or param_0067:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        end
        return nil
    end
    function SetupBringItems()
        StopBringItems()
        if _Unloading then
            return
        end
        if not local_0122.BringItems["Value"] then
            return
        end
        local local_0192 = 639796 - 639796
        BringItemsConn = service_run_service_0047.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if _Unloading then
                return
            end
            if not local_0122.BringItems["Value"] then
                return
            end
            if CurrentFloor == "Fools" then
                return
            end
            if tick() < (ScrapDroppedItemsHoldUntil or 523888 + -523888) then
                ClearBringItemsForces()
                return
            end
            local local_0193 = local_0115 or local_player_0050.Character
            local local_0194 = local_0193 and local_0193:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local camera_0094 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if
                not local_0194
                or not camera_0094
                or local_0194.Health <= 1344672000 % 7308000
                or local_0194:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType["Dead"]
            then
                ClearBringItemsForces()
                return
            end
            local local_0195 = (
                camera_0094.CFrame["Position"] + camera_0094.CFrame["LookVector"] * 2
            ) + Vector3.new(-652724 + 652724, -0.4, 679349 + -679349)
            local child_instance_0095 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Drops")
            if not child_instance_0095 then
                return
            end
            local local_0196 = 393004 + -393004
            for item_0081, item_0082 in ipairs(child_instance_0095:GetChildren()) do
                local local_0197 = ResolveDropItem(item_0082)
                if not local_0197 then
                    continue
                end
                local_0196 += -711029 - -711030
                if local_0197.Anchored then
                    pcall(function()
                        local_0197.Anchored = false
                    end)
                end
                local local_0198 = local_0197:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyVelocity")
                if not local_0198 then
                    local_0198 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    local_0198.Name = "BringItemsVelocity"
                    local_0198.MaxForce = Vector3.new(
                        1829530138678 % 9012525439,
                        415021 + 8999584979,
                        9000741175 - 741175
                    )
                    local_0198.Parent = local_0197
                    table.insert(BringItemsBVs, local_0198)
                end
                local local_0199 = local_0195 - local_0197.Position
                if local_0199.Magnitude > 2329563086 % 12391293 then
                    local_0197.CFrame = local_0197.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(local_0195), 0.4)
                end
                local_0198.Velocity = local_0199 * 40
            end
            local local_0200 = os.clock()
            if local_0200 - local_0192 >= 3437699683 % 15011789 then
                local_0192 = local_0200
            end
        end)
    end

    function HotelAssistCollect(param_0118, param_0119, param_0120, param_0121)
        local local_0280 = os.clock() + (param_0121 or -430906)
        while not _Unloading do
            if HotelAssistRoom() ~= param_0119 then
                return false
            end
            if param_0120 and param_0120() then
                return true
            end
            if not param_0118 or not param_0118.Parent then
                return true
            end
            if os.clock() > local_0280 then
                return false
            end
            if HotelAssistTP(param_0118) then
                HotelAssistFire(param_0118)
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        return false
    end

    function HotelAutoLibrary()
        local child_instance_0155 = workspace:FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
        if not child_instance_0155 then
            return
        end
        local child_instance_0156 = child_instance_0155:FindFirstChild("50")
        if not child_instance_0156 then
            return
        end
        local local_0281 = 602808 - 602803
        local local_0282 = local_0125.HotelBooksCollected or 30221240 % 888860
        local local_0283 = os.clock() + (508080 + -507900)
        while local_0282 < local_0281 and (os.clock() < local_0283 and not _Unloading) do
            if HotelAssistRoom() ~= -235111 + 235161 then
                return
            end
            local local_0284
            for item_0171, item_0172 in ipairs(child_instance_0156:GetDescendants()) do
                if item_0172.Name == "LiveHintBook" then
                    local_0284 = item_0172
                    break
                end
            end
            if not local_0284 then
                task.wait(0.1)
            elseif HotelAssistCollect(local_0284, 566489490 % 5149904) then
                local_0282 += 846010 + -846009
                local_0125.HotelBooksCollected = local_0282
            else
                if HotelAssistRoom() ~= 401668808 % 11813787 or _Unloading then
                    return
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end
        if local_0282 < local_0281 then
            return false
        end
        local local_0285 = os.clock() + 60
        while os.clock() < local_0285 and not _Unloading do
            if HotelAssistRoom() ~= 139556 + -139506 then
                return false
            end
            if HotelAssistHasPaper() then
                return true
            end
            local child_instance_0157 = child_instance_0156:FindFirstChild("LibraryHintPaper", true)
            if not child_instance_0157 then
                task.wait(0.1)
            else
                HotelAssistCollect(child_instance_0157, 11631042 % 47668, HotelAssistHasPaper, 10)
            end
        end
        return HotelAssistHasPaper()
    end

    function CheesyQueueOnTeleport()
        local local_0296 = queue_on_teleport or queueonteleport
        if type(local_0296) ~= "function" then

                ["Title"] = "Queue On Teleport",
                ["Description"] = "Your executor does not support queue_on_teleport",
                ["Time"] = -999743 + 999748,
            })
            return false
        end
        local pcall_success_0163, local_0297 = pcall(function()
            local_0296('loadstring(game:HttpGet("' .. (QueueOnTeleportURL .. '"))()'))
        end)
        if not pcall_success_0163 then
            ui_library:Notify({
                ["Title"] = "Queue On Teleport",
                ["Description"] = tostring(local_0297),
                ["Time"] = 1012229 - 1012223,
            })
            return false
        end
        ui_library:Notify({
            ["Title"] = "Queue On Teleport",
            ["Description"] = "Queued. It will re-run when you rejoin.",
            ["Time"] = 3966197285 % 16525822,
        })
        return true
    end

    function IsTombObject(param_0190)
        if not param_0190 or not param_0190.Parent then
            return false
        end
        local child_instance_0234 = param_0190:FindFirstChild("Lid")
        if not child_instance_0234 then
            return false
        end

    EntityPathNodes = {}
    EntityPathConns = {}
    EntityPathContainers = setmetatable({}, { ["__mode"] = "k" })
    local function helper_0100(param_0207)
        if typeof(param_0207) ~= "Instance" then
            return false
        end
        if not (param_0207:IsA("Folder") or param_0207:IsA("Model")) then
            return false
        end
        return (string.lower(param_0207.Name)):find("node") ~= nil
    end
    function ClearEntityPath()
        for item_0292, item_0293 in ipairs(EntityPathNodes) do
            if item_0293 and item_0293.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    item_0293.Transparency = 1451767429 % 7116507
                end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(EntityPathNodes)
        for item_0294, item_0295 in pairs(EntityPathConns) do
            pcall(item_0295.Disconnect, item_0295)
        end
        table.clear(EntityPathConns)
        table.clear(EntityPathContainers)
        if Connections.EntityPathWatch then
            pcall(Connections.EntityPathWatch["Disconnect"], Connections.EntityPathWatch)
            Connections.EntityPathWatch = nil
        end
        if Connections.EntityPathRooms then
            pcall(Connections.EntityPathRooms["Disconnect"], Connections.EntityPathRooms)
            Connections.EntityPathRooms = nil
        end
    end
    function ApplyEntityPath(param_0208)
        if typeof(param_0208) ~= "Instance" then
            return
        end
        if not param_0208:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end
        if param_0208.Transparency == -997375 - -997375 then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            param_0208.Transparency = -301929 - -301929
        end)
        table.insert(EntityPathNodes, param_0208)
    end
    function WatchEntityPathContainer(param_0209)
        if EntityPathContainers[param_0209] then
            return
        end
        EntityPathContainers[param_0209] = true
        for item_0296, item_0297 in ipairs(param_0209:GetDescendants()) do
            ApplyEntityPath(item_0297)
        end
        EntityPathConns[param_0209] = param_0209.DescendantAdded:Connect(ApplyEntityPath)
    end
    function InEntityPathContainer(param_0210)
        local local_0566 = param_0210.Parent
        while local_0566 and local_0566 ~= child_instance_0052 do
            if EntityPathContainers[local_0566] or helper_0100(local_0566) then
                return true
            end
            local_0566 = local_0566.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    function RebuildEntityPath()
        ClearEntityPath()
        if not local_0122.ShowEntityPath or not local_0122.ShowEntityPath["Value"] then
            return
        end
        if not child_instance_0052 then
            return
        end
        for item_0298, item_0299 in ipairs(child_instance_0052:GetDescendants()) do
            if helper_0100(item_0299) then
                WatchEntityPathContainer(item_0299)
            elseif item_0299:IsA("BasePart") and InEntityPathContainer(item_0299) then
                ApplyEntityPath(item_0299)
            end
        end
        Connections.EntityPathWatch = child_instance_0052.DescendantAdded:Connect(
            function(param_0211)
                if typeof(param_0211) ~= "Instance" then
                    return
                end
                if helper_0100(param_0211) then
                    WatchEntityPathContainer(param_0211)
                elseif param_0211:IsA("BasePart") and InEntityPathContainer(param_0211) then
                    ApplyEntityPath(param_0211)
                end
            end
        )
    end
    Groupboxes.Visuals_ESP:AddToggle("ShowEntityPath", {
        ["Text"] = "Show Entity Path",
        ["Default"] = false,
        ["Tooltip"] = "Shows the pathfind nodes entities follow in each room",
        ["Callback"] = function(param_0212)
            RebuildEntityPath()
        end,
    })
    if local_0122.ShowEntityPath and local_0122.ShowEntityPath["Value"] then
        task.defer(RebuildEntityPath)
    end

    function GetLatestClock()
        local local_0602, local_0603 = nil, 725366 + -725367
        local child_instance_0273 = (game:GetService("Workspace")):FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
        if not child_instance_0273 then
            return nil
        end
        for item_0322, item_0323 in ipairs(child_instance_0273:GetChildren()) do
            local local_0604 = tonumber(item_0323.Name)
            if local_0604 and local_0604 >= local_0603 then
                local child_instance_0274 = item_0323:FindFirstChild("Assets")
                local local_0605 = child_instance_0274
                    and child_instance_0274:FindFirstChild("ArchivesClock")
                if local_0605 then
                    local_0602, local_0603 = local_0605, local_0604
                end
            end
        end
        return local_0602
    end
    function FormatClock(param_0220)
        local local_0606 = math.floor(param_0220 / (943504055 % 11100047))
        local local_0607 = param_0220 % (1008635 - 1008575)
        local local_0608 = local_0606 >= -623783 - -623795 and "PM" or "AM"
        local local_0609 = local_0606 % 12
        if local_0609 == -193382 + 193382 then
            local_0609 = 217767 + -217755
        end
        return string.format("%d:%02d %s", local_0609, local_0607, local_0608)
    end

    function ResolveKillTarget(param_0230)

                        303922477 % 1948221,

    end

    function UpdateKillChairConn()
        if KillChairConn then
            KillChairConn:Disconnect()
            KillChairConn = nil
        end
        local local_0635 = local_0122.StairwellKillPlayer
            and local_0122.StairwellKillPlayer["Value"]

                and local_0123.StairwellKillMethod["Value"]
            or "Shopping Cart"
        local local_0640 = local_0639 == "Chair"
        local local_0641 = local_0640 and (local_0635 or local_0636)
        local local_0642 = local_0637 or local_0638
        if not (local_0641 or local_0642) then
            StopKillChairForces()
            return
        end
        KillChairConn = service_run_service_0047.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if local_0635 and local_0640 then
                local local_0643 = ResolveKillTarget(local_0123.StairwellKillTarget)
                if local_0643 then
                    ApplyKillChairForceFor(local_0643)
                end
            end
            if local_0637 then
                local local_0644 = ResolveKillTarget(local_0123.ArchivesKillTarget)
                if local_0644 then
                    ApplyKillChairForceFor(local_0644)
                end
            end
            if local_0636 and local_0640 then
                local local_0645 = GetKillAlivePlayers()
                if #local_0645 > 783227865 % 5053083 then
                    if KillAllIndex.Stairwell > #local_0645 then
                        KillAllIndex.Stairwell = 674364601 % 10704200
                    end
                    ApplyKillChairForceFor(local_0645[KillAllIndex.Stairwell])
                    KillAllIndex.Stairwell = KillAllIndex.Stairwell + 110905705 % 2843736
                end
            end
            if local_0638 then
                local local_0646 = GetKillAlivePlayers()
                if #local_0646 > 681909 + -681909 then
                    if KillAllIndex.Archives > #local_0646 then
                        KillAllIndex.Archives = 820985 + -820984
                    end
                    ApplyKillChairForceFor(local_0646[KillAllIndex.Archives])
                    KillAllIndex.Archives = KillAllIndex.Archives + (381612 + -381611)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function StripCrusherParts(param_0244)
        if not param_0244 then
            return
        end
        for item_0365, item_0366 in ipairs(param_0244:GetDescendants()) do
            if item_0366:IsA("BasePart") then
                if not IsCrusherUnderBasicWall(item_0366, param_0244) then
                    item_0366.CanCollide = false
                    item_0366.CanTouch = false
                end
            end
            if item_0366.Name == "StairwellCrusher" then
                for item_0367, item_0368 in ipairs({ "OuterTrigger", "CoreTrigger" }) do
                    local child_instance_0303 = item_0366:FindFirstChild(item_0368)
                    if child_instance_0303 then
                        pcall(function()
                            child_instance_0303:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if CrusherConns[param_0244] then
            return
        end
        CrusherConns[param_0244] = param_0244.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(param_0245)
            if
                param_0245:IsA("BasePart") and not IsCrusherUnderBasicWall(param_0245, param_0244)
            then
                param_0245.CanCollide = false
                param_0245.CanTouch = false
            end
        end)
    end
    function ScanCrushers()
        local child_instance_0304 = workspace:FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
        if not child_instance_0304 then
            return
        end
        for item_0369, item_0370 in ipairs(child_instance_0304:GetChildren()) do
            local child_instance_0305 = item_0370:FindFirstChild("Assets")
            if child_instance_0305 then
                for item_0371, item_0372 in ipairs(child_instance_0305:GetChildren()) do
                    if item_0372.Name:match("^StairwellCrusherContainer%d+$") then
                        StripCrusherParts(item_0372)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function StopAntiCrusher()
        if AntiCrusherConn then
            AntiCrusherConn:Disconnect()

                pcall(function()
                    item_0374:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
            CrusherConns[item_0373] = nil
        end
    end

    function Functions.HotelAutoWalk.TeleportStep()

    function Functions.HotelAutoWalk.RunTeleport()
        if local_0125.HotelTeleportThread then
            return
        end
        local_0125.HotelTeleportThread = task.spawn(function()
            while local_0122.HotelAutoWalk["Value"] and HotelMethodName() == "Teleport" do
                pcall(function()
                    Functions.HotelAutoWalk["TeleportStep"]()
                end)
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
            local_0125.HotelTeleportThread = nil
        end)
    end
    function Functions.HotelAutoWalk.StopTeleport()

            end)
            local_0125.HotelTeleportThread = nil
        end
        local_0125.HotelTpRoom = nil
        local_0125.HotelTpStage = nil
    end

    local function helper_0143(param_0318)
        local function helper_0144(param_0319, param_0320)
            local local_0857 = ((param_0319.CFrame + param_0319.CFrame["LookVector"]))["Position"]
            local local_0858 = (
                (param_0319.CFrame + param_0319.CFrame["LookVector"] * (-12128 + 12127))
            )["Position"]
            return ((local_0857 - param_0320.Position))["Magnitude"]
                > ((local_0858 - param_0320.Position))["Magnitude"]
        end
        local function helper_0145(param_0321, param_0322)
            local local_0859 =
                param_0321.CFrame["RightVector"]:Dot(param_0321.Position - param_0322.Position)
            if local_0859 > 133476.5 - 133476 then
                return helper_0144(param_0321, param_0322) and "Right" or "Left"
            end
            if local_0859 < 425837 + -425837.5 then
                return helper_0144(param_0321, param_0322) and "Left" or "Right"
            end
            return "Straight"
        end
        local function helper_0146(param_0323)
            local local_0860, local_0861 = nil, math.huge

                if
                    item_0537 ~= param_0323
                    and (local_0863 and (local_0862 and local_0863 > local_0862))

                    if
                        local_0864 < local_0861
                        and item_0537:GetAttribute("DistanceBlacklist") ~= true

                        local_0861 = local_0864
                        local_0860 = item_0537

    _mgPending = false

    local function helper_0154()
        if local_0916 then
            return local_0916
        end
        if isSolara() then
            return nil
        end
        if _mgPending or tick() - _mgLastTry < 639545 - 639540 then
            return nil
        end
        _mgLastTry = tick()
        _mgPending = true
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local local_0917 = local_player_0050:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                local local_0918 = local_0917 and local_0917:FindFirstChild("MainUI")
                local local_0919 = local_0918 and local_0918:FindFirstChild("Initiator")
                local local_0920 = local_0919 and local_0919:FindFirstChild("Main_Game")
                if local_0920 then
                    local pcall_success_0414, local_0921 = pcall(require, local_0920)
                    if pcall_success_0414 and type(local_0921) == "table" then
                        local_0916 = local_0921
                    end
                end
            end)
            _mgPending = false
        end)
        return nil
    end
